            CFDLean.JST1.LamOn vb cb bnd n i

    (∀ (α : Fin d), 0 < h α) →
      let sgn := fun s => if s = Bool.true then 1 else -1;
      let E := fun p => (sgn p.2 * h p.1) • Pi.single p.1 1;
      let Y := fun p => (sgn p.2 * (V / h p.1)) • Pi.single p.1 1;

              CFDLean.Num.outer (CFDLean.Num.WMesh.unit (E p)) (CFDLean.Num.WMesh.unit (E p)) =
          (2 * V) • 1

STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P2_6_3 :: ∀ (ι C : Type) [Fintype ι] [inst : DecidableEq ι] [inst_1 : Fintype C] (d : ℕ)
  (M : CFDLean.Num.WMesh ι C d) (bd : C × Fin (d + 1) → Bool),
  M.Valid →
    1 ≤ d →
      M.Conforming bd →
        (∀ (i : ι), M.beta i = ∑ p, if bd p = Bool.true ∧ i ∈ M.facetNodes p then (1 / ↑d) • M.areaVec p else 0) ∧
          (∀ (i k : ι),

                ∑ p,

  (β : ι → CFDLean.Num.Vec d) (F : ι → Matrix κ (Fin d) ℝ),
  (∀ (i : ι), ∑ k, c i k = 0) →
    (∀ (k : ι), ∑ i, c i k = β k) →
      (∀ (i : ι), ∑ k, B i k = β i) →
        let n := fun i k => c i k - c k i;
        let s := fun i k => (1 / 2) • (c i k + c k i - B i k);

            ∑ k, (F k).mulVec (c i k)

      (∀ (k : ι), 0 < CFDLean.Num.dot (y k) (e k)) →

                0 ≤ (1 - 1 / c) * CFDLean.JST1.Wall.Dgal T K w)

STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P2_10_4 :: ∀ (d : ℕ) (D : CFDLean.JST1.SSTPoint.Data d),
  0 < D.R →
    0 < D.T →
      let θ := CFDLean.JST1.SSTPoint.theta D;
      (CFDLean.JST1.SSTPoint.Pi D =
          -θ * (D.μ * CFDLean.JST1.SSTPoint.Q D + (D.μt * CFDLean.JST1.SSTPoint.Q D - D.Pk) + D.ε) -

STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P2_10_5 :: ∀ (ι : Type) [inst : Fintype ι] (mdot : ι → ι → ℝ) (kv kin bmass : ι → ℝ),
  (∀ (i j : ι), mdot j i = -mdot i j) →
    let kup := fun i j => if 0 ≤ mdot i j then kv i else kv j;
    let kb := fun i => if 0 ≤ bmass i then kv i else kin i;
    let X := fun i => ∑ j, mdot i j * kup i j + bmass i * kb i;
    let Rrho := fun i => ∑ j, mdot i j + bmass i;
    ∑ i, ∑ j, mdot i j * kup i j = 0 ∧
      (∀ (k0 : ℝ), (∀ (i : ι), kv i = k0) → (∀ (i : ι), kin i = k0) → ∀ (i : ι), X i = k0 * Rrho i) ∧
        ∀ (i : ι), kv i = 0 → bmass i = 0 → (∀ (j : ι), 0 ≤ kv j) → X i ≤ 0

STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P2_10_7 :: ∀ (d : ℕ) (R μ μt κ : ℝ) (wb : CFDLean.JST1.State d)
  (G : Matrix (CFDLean.JST1.Channel d) (Fin d) ℝ),
  d ≤ 3 →
    0 < R →
      0 ≤ μ →
        0 ≤ μt →
          0 ≤ κ →
            wb CFDLean.JST1.Channel.energy < 0 →

STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P2_10_8 :: (∀ (ι C : Type) [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : Fintype C] (d : ℕ)
    (w Rhat : ι → CFDLean.JST1.State d) (X Ck Bk Dh Sk Rrho Pst V β2 : ι → ℝ) (inD : ι → Bool) (kD : ℝ)
    (Dgal Dt PiW : C → ℝ) (Bhat Pi2 Pi4 : ℝ),
    (∀ (i : ι), w i CFDLean.JST1.Channel.energy = -β2 i) →
      (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ β2 i) →
        (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ V i) →
          (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ Pst i) →
            -∑ i, CFDLean.Num.pairF (w i) (Rhat i) ≤ Bhat + Pi2 + Pi4 - ∑ T, (Dgal T - PiW T) →
              Pi2 ≤ 0 →
                (∀ (i : ι), inD i = Bool.false → X i = Ck i + Bk i + Dh i) →
                  (∀ (i : ι), inD i = Bool.false → Sk i ≤ Pst i) →
                    (∀ (i : ι), inD i = Bool.true → X i - kD * Rrho i ≤ 0) →
                      ∑ T, Dt T = ∑ i, β2 i * V i * Pst i →
                        (∀ (T : C), PiW T ≤ Dgal T - Dt T) →
                          let Rk := fun i => if inD i = Bool.true then kD * Rrho i else Ck i + Bk i + Dh i - V i * Sk i;
                          let rate := -∑ i, CFDLean.Num.pairF (w i) (Rhat i + (X i - Rk i) • CFDLean.JST1.eE);
                          (rate ≤
                              (Bhat + Pi2 + Pi4 - ∑ T, (Dgal T - Dt T - PiW T) -
                                  ∑ i, if inD i = Bool.true then 0 else β2 i * V i * (Pst i - Sk i)) -
                                ∑ i, if inD i = Bool.true then β2 i * V i * Pst i else 0) ∧
                            (∀ (T : C), 0 ≤ Dgal T - Dt T - PiW T) ∧
                              (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ if inD i = Bool.true then 0 else β2 i * V i * (Pst i - Sk i)) ∧
                                ∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ if inD i = Bool.true then β2 i * V i * Pst i else 0) ∧
  ∀ (Dgal Dt PiW c : ℝ),
    (PiW = 0 → Dt ≤ Dgal → PiW ≤ Dgal - Dt) ∧ (0 < c → PiW ≤ Dgal / c → Dt ≤ (1 - 1 / c) * Dgal → PiW ≤ Dgal - Dt)

STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P2_10_8b :: ∀ (d : ℕ) (T : CFDLean.Num.Simplex d) (R μ μt κ c : ℝ)
  (P : Matrix (CFDLean.JST1.Channel d) (CFDLean.JST1.Channel d) ℝ) (gw : CFDLean.JST1.State d)

STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P3_14_1 :: ∀ (ι C Q : Type) [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] [inst_2 : Fintype C]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq C] [inst_4 : Fintype Q] (d : ℕ) (S : CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Data ι C Q d)
  (bd : C × Fin (d + 1) → Bool) (fq : Q → C × Fin (d + 1)) (aq : Q → ℝ) (u : ι → CFDLean.JST1.State d),
  CFDLean.JST1.Spec.SchemeHyp S bd fq aq u →
    ∑ i, CFDLean.Num.pairF (CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.w S u i) (CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.resid S u i) =
        CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Bterm S + CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Pi2 S u + CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Pi4 S u -
            ∑ T, S.M.ω T * (CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Dg S u T - CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Pw S u T) +
          CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.wallFlux S u ∧
      CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Pi2 S u ≤ 0 ∧
        (1 ≤ S.c →
            ∀ (T : C),
              (1 - 1 / S.c) * CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Dg S u T ≤
                  CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Dg S u T - CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Pw S u T ∧
                0 ≤ (1 - 1 / S.c) * CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Dg S u T) ∧
          ∀ (i k : ι),
            CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.central S u k i + CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.diss2 S u k i -
                CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.diss4 S u k i =
              -(CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.central S u i k + CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.diss2 S u i k -
                  CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.diss4 S u i k)

  (∀ (q : Q), CFDLean.JST1.Admissible S.g (S.ut q)) →

                CFDLean.Num.pairF (CFDLean.JST1.entropyVars S.g (ug q)) (S.Fs q)
STMT CFDLean.JST1.Spec.P3_14_3 :: ∀ (ι C Q : Type) [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] [inst_2 : Fintype C]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq C] [inst_4 : Fintype Q] (d : ℕ) (S : CFDLean.JST1.Scheme.Data ι C Q d)
  (bd : C × Fin (d + 1) → Bool) (fq : Q → C × Fin (d + 1)) (aq : Q → ℝ) (Ut : ι → ℝ → CFDLean.JST1.SState d)
  (Vv : ι → ℝ) (R : ι → CFDLean.JST1.SState d) (t : ℝ) (X Ck Bk Dh Sk Rrho Pst : ι → ℝ) (inD : ι → Bool) (kD : ℝ)
  (Dt : C → ℝ),

  CFDLean.JST1.Spec.SchemeHyp S bd fq aq u →

                (∀ (i : ι), inD i = Bool.true → X i - kD * Rrho i ≤ 0) →
                  ∑ T, S.M.ω T * Dt T = ∑ i, β2 i * Vv i * Pst i →

                                ∑ i, if inD i = Bool.true then 0 else β2 i * Vv i * (Pst i - Sk i)) -
                              ∑ i, if inD i = Bool.true then β2 i * Vv i * Pst i else 0
